def project_box_sum(h, target):
    h=np.clip(h,0,1)
    if abs(h.sum()-target)<1e-12: return h
    lo,hi = -1.0 - h.max(), 1.0 - h.min()
    # want g(lambda)=sum(clip(h-lambda,0,1))-target. As lambda increases, g decreases.
```

Pair search algorithm:
Input h feasible, target n/2. Compute F = np.correlate(h,1-h) counts (full length L=2n-1); current max M = F.max(). We want find pair δ reducing M. We can iterate:
- Compute weights w over lags with positive margin: e.g., w_l = max(0, M - F_l + tol) maybe active. But for gradient of max, true subgradient is any convex combo of active lags. Use smooth weights exp(α(F-M)).
- Compute g (gradient of weighted sum) length n:
For lag l (index idx), weight w. For each lag, need add w * (Δ_i? Actually gradient of F_l wrt h_k = Δ_k(l) with coefficient 1? Wait F_l = ∑ h_{p+l}(1-h_p). Derivative wrt h_k = as h factor (1-h_{k-l}) + as complement -h_{k+l}. That's Δ_k(l). Yes. So g_k = ∑_l w_l Δ_k(l). We can compute efficiently using convolutions/cumsums.
For each lag l, contribution to g:
  For p valid, term h_{p+l}(1-h_p). Derivative w.r.t p as complement and p+l as h. So g[p] += -w h_{p+l}; g[p+l] += w(1-h_p). For l=0, g[p]+= -w h_p + w(1-h_p)=w(1-2h_p).
This is like adding weighted cross-correlations. We can compute g by np.correlate of w? Actually for each l, h_{p+l} and h_p. Total linear part of product? g = ∑ w_l h shifted? Let's derive using F_l = ∑ h_{p+l} - h_{p+l}h_p. For l≠0, ∂/∂h_k = ∑_{l} w_l [ (1 if k is h factor) + ...], but also linear sum. Could compute directly with loops over lags? L~500, for each do slices O(n) ->250*500=125k, okay per gradient. We can vectorize loops over lags in Python 500 loops, each slice operations of avg length 125 -> 62k total, fine. 500 Python loops okay. Do this per iteration maybe 100 times -> 6M operations. Good.
Simpler gradient function:
```
def grad_weighted(h, F, w): # w length L aligned correlate lags
   n=len(h); g=np.zeros(n); L=len(w); offset=n-1
   for idx,wl in enumerate(w):
      if wl==0: continue
      l=idx-offset
      if l>0:
         # p=0..n-1-l
         p=np.arange? avoid. Use slices:
         # derivative for p as complement: g[0:n-l] += -wl * h[l:n]
         # derivative for p+l as h: g[l:n] += wl*(1-h[0:n-l])
      elif l==0:
         g += wl*(1-2*h)
      else: # l<0, p from -l to n-1? term h_{p+l} with p+l valid; p range -l..n-1
         # p start=-l, len=n+l? since l negative
         start=-l; end=n
         # h_factor at p+l: indices start+l=0 .. end+l-1 = n+l-1 length n+l
         # complement p: start..end-1
         m=n+l
         g[start:end] += -wl * h[start+l:end+l]  # h[0:n+l]
         g[0:n+l] += wl*(1-h[start:end])  # h[-l:n]
      # But double count l=0? handle separately.
```
Check l>0: p=0..n-1-l, h_factor index p+l = l..n-1 length n-l; complement p=0..n-1-l. Add -wl*h[l:n] to g[0:n-l]; add wl*(1-h[0:n-l]) to g[l:n].
For l<0: l=-a. p=a..n-1, h_factor index p-a =0..n-a-1=n+l-1; complement p=a..n-1. Add -wl*h[0:n+l] to g[a:n]; add wl*(1-h[a:n]) to g[0:n+l].
For l=0: g += wl(1-2h).
This gives gradient of ∑ w_l F_l? Let's test l=1: derivative wrt k as h at p=k-1 if k-1≥0: (1-h_{k-1}); as complement k if k≤n-2: -h_{k+1}. Code: for l=1, g[0:n-1] += -h[1:n] (complement); g[1:n] += 1-h[0:n-1] (h). Good. For l=-1: g[1:n] += -h[0:n-1] (complement p); g[0:n-1] += 1-h[1:n] (h factor). Good.

Implement:
```
def delta_k(k,h,n,L,offset):
  d=np.zeros(L)
  # comp
  l0=max(-(n-1), -k); l1=min(n-1, n-1-k)
  if l0<=l1:
     idx=l0+offset + np.arange(l1-l0+1)
     d[idx] += -h[k + (idx-offset)]
  # hfact
  l0=max(-(n-1), k-(n-1)); l1=min(n-1,k)
  if l0<=l1:
     idx=l0+offset+...
     d[idx] += 1 - h[k - (idx-offset)]
  return d
```

def smooth_optimize(h, n, deadline, max_iter=2000):
  h = project_box_sum(h.copy(),target)
  beta? Use h variables.
  step=1.0
  for alpha in [10,50,200,1000]:
    for it in ...:
       F=counts; s=softmax(alpha*(F-F.max()))
       g=grad_weights(h,s)
       g -= g.mean() # tangent to sum
       # line search: try step=1,0.5,... compute phi(h-step*gproj)
       gp = project_box_sum(h - step*g)

       else: step*=0.5
       stop if step small

### Projection
```
def project_box_sum(h, target):
    h = np.asarray(h, dtype=float)
    h = np.clip(h,0,1)
    s = h.sum()
    if abs(s-target) < 1e-12: return h
    lo = -1.0 - h.max() # too low means clip all 1? Actually subtract λ.
    hi = 1.0 - h.min()
    # g(λ)=sum(clip(h-λ,0,1))-target, decreasing in λ
    # If h sum > target, need increase λ (lower values). hi such that sum 0? λ>max -> 0. Choose hi=1.1? Since h≤1, λ=1 gives 0. low=-1 gives 2? For h in [0,1], λ=1 =>0, λ=-1 => all 1 (sum n). So bracket [-1,1] enough.
    lo,hi=-1.0,1.0
    # ensure g(lo)>=target>=g(hi)
    for _ in range(60):
       mid=0.5*(lo+hi)
       val=np.sum(np.clip(h-mid,0,1))
       if val > target: lo=mid else: hi=mid
    p=np.clip(h-0.5*(lo+hi),0,1)
    diff=target-p.sum()
    if abs(diff)>1e-14:
       # adjust among slack
       # if diff>0 add to values not 1; else subtract not 0
       if diff>0:
          idx=np.argmax(1-p)
          p[idx]=min(1,p[idx]+diff)
          # if not enough? should due target feasible.
       else:
          idx=np.argmax(p)
          p[idx]=max(0,p[idx]+diff)
       # if still not exact due multiple? use iterative small
    # ensure exact in floating by last: p = np.round? no.
    return p
```
But bisection on [-1,1] after clipping h may not have exact target if target outside [0,n]? target=n/2 within. Good.
Need exact sum to n/2 maybe if n odd target .5. Adjusting by diff could exceed bound if diff large; our bisection close.

### Smooth objective and gradient
For LSE gradient, implement with loops over lags using weights w (sum maybe 1). For speed, skip weights below eps. Use F values for gradient? gradient independent of F. But weights require F counts. We'll compute F anyway for current value.
```
def weighted_grad(h,w):
   n=len(h); L=len(w); off=n-1; g=np.zeros(n)
   for idx,wl in enumerate(w):
      if wl <= 1e-15: continue
      l=idx-off
      if l>0:
         m=n-l
         g[:m] -= wl*h[l:]
         g[l:] += wl*(1-h[:m])
      elif l==0:
         g += wl*(1-2*h)
      else:
         m=n+l  # l negative, m = n-l? For l=-a, m=n-a
         # p gives comp at p a..n, h[-h_p] -> g[p]
         g[-l:]? start=-l
         g[-l:] -= wl*h[:m]
         g[:m] += wl*(1-h[-l:])
   return g
```
Check l<0: l=-a, m=n-a. start=a. p=a..n-1 length m. h_factor indices p-a=0..m-1. comp g[p] += -wl*h[p-a] => g[start:] -= wl*h[:m]. h_factor g[p-a] += wl*(1-h[p]) => g[:m] += wl*(1-h[start:]). Good.
For l>0: m=n-l. comp g[:m] -= wl*h[l:]; hfac g[l:] += wl*(1-h[:m]). Good.
This computes gradient of ∑w F_l. If weights sum 1, scale approx n? Actually each F_l sum, gradient entries O(length). Good.

Smooth value:
```
def smooth_value_gradient(h, alpha):
   F=np.correlate(h,1-h,'full')
   m=F.max()
   z=alpha*(F-m)
   ez=np.exp(z)
   s=ez/ez.sum()
   val=m + np.log(ez.sum())/alpha
   g=weighted_grad(h,s)
   return val,g
```
Need avoid overflow if alpha*diff large negative/positive; F-m≤0 so exp ok. If α up to 1000, min exp 0 okay.

Pseudo:
```
def pair_search(h, deadline, max_moves=100000, rng=None, grid=48, min_improve_c=1e-10):
    if rng is None: rng=np.random.RandomState(0)
    n=len(h); target=n/2; dx=2/n
    h=project_box_sum(h.copy(), target)
    F=np.correlate(h,1-h,'full')
    M=F.max(); C=M*dx
    best_h=h.copy(); best_F=F.copy(); bestC=C
    L=lens? off=n-1
    no_improve=0
    moves=0
    grace_deadline=deadline
    while time.time() < deadline and moves < max_moves:
        # gradient active weights:
        # active if F > M - active_margin. Use margin = max(1e-8, 1e-4*M?) maybe.
        # For smooth weights, alpha_pair = 20? counts scale ~50, to weight active cluster.
        alpha_p = 30.0
        z=alpha_p*(F-M); ez=np.exp(z); w=ez/ez.sum()
        g=weighted_grad(h,w)
        # sort
        order=np.argsort(g)
        P = 6 # maybe 5
        low=order[:P]; high=order[-P:][::-1]
        # Build candidate list, store to avoid duplicates
        cand=[]
        for i in low:
           for j in high:
              if i!=j: cand.append((i,j))
        # random candidate pairs based on g quantiles
        for _ in range(10+P):
           i = order[rng.randint(0,P+10)]
           j = order[rng.randint(n-P-10,n-P-1)]
           if i!=j: cand.append((i,j))
        # If no improved in cand, try larger random all pairs?
        improved=False
        for i,j in cand:
            if time.time() > deadline: break
            # If too many candidates no improve, maybe break after first no improve? We'll evaluate all.
            lo = max(-h[i], h[j]-1.0)
            hi = min(h[j], 1.0 - h[i])
            if hi - lo < 1e-13: continue
            # quick linear? Evaluate grid
            d, val = best_pair_move(F, i,j,h, lo,hi, M, grid)
            if val is not None and val < C - min_improve_c? val counts. compare val*dx < bestC - ... but current M maybe best? We maintain bestC. use target_val = (bestC - min_improve_c)/dx? But M current maybe =bestC/dx. If no improvement in current, use M.
                apply:
                h[i]=clamp...; h[j]=...
                F = F + Lcoeff*d + B*d*d  (best_pair_move can return Lcoeff,B,d)
                M = F.max()
                if M*dx < bestC: update best
                improved=True
                moves+=1
                break
        if improved:
            no_improve=0
        else:
            no_improve += 1
            # try some random far pairs not based on g, with small grid maybe
            if no_improve > 20:
                # do 50 random pairs
                for _ in range(80): ...
                if improved break
            if no_improve > 200:
                # perturb h slightly preserving sum to escape, recompute F
                # choose random i,j, delta = 0.02*random sign within interval, update, recompute F exact (not accept based on improvement)
                # accept only if improves a lot? For escape, just perturb and recompute, even if worse, to continue search? Local search can use stochastic acceptance.
                if no_improve > 500:
                    # random perturbation and recompute
                    i=rng.randint(n); j=rng.randint(n); if i!=j: choose delta small 0.05 in random direction feasible
                    h[i]=clip; h[j]=clip; h=project_box_sum(h,target); F=...; M=...
                    no_improve=0
                else:
                    break
            if no_improve > 1000: break
    # final: maybe recompute weights active exact and pair search with larger P? but time left
    h=project_box_sum(h,target)
    C=compute_c5(h,n)
    return h,C
```

### Implementing annealing pair moves
```
def pair_anneal(h, deadline, rng, T_start=1e-3, T_end=1e-7):
    h project; F counts; M; C
    bestC=C; best_h
    temperature=T_start; moves=0
    while time<deadline:
       if moves %100==0: temperature=T_start*(T_end/T_start)**(moves/max_moves) or based on time
       # Candidate pairs: use random low/g? For anneal, random pairs.
       i=rng.randint(n); j=rng.randint(n)
       if i==j: continue
       lo,hi...
       # sample δ: if rng <0.2: best δ via grid (but expensive). For speed, sample δ randomly in [lo,hi] with bias to 0: δ = rng.uniform(0,1)* (hi-lo)*0.2 * sign
       d = 0.2 * (hi-lo) * rng.randn? clipped to [lo,hi]
       # compute val = max(F + Ld + Bd^2) using L from delta_i-delta_j.
       if val*dx < C + temperature:
           accept: update
           if newC<bestC record
       moves++
```
This uses O(n) for L + vector max (500) per move. Could do many moves. But grid best is more effective. We can use best δ sometimes. Simulated annealing with random small moves may be slow. Skip for now unless time.

### SLSQP smoothed alpha
If we use SLSQP on h variables, objective counts scaled. It may move h across many variables rather than pair. Good. Let's implement safe function:
```
def slsqp_smooth(h, n, alpha, maxiter, ftol=1e-14):
    h = project_box_sum(hCopy)
    _STATE.update(...)
    res = minimize(slsqp_fun, h, method='SLSQP', bounds=..., constraints=..., jac=slsqp_jac, options={'maxiter':maxiter,'ftol':ftol})
    return project_box_sum(res.x,n/2) if success/finish else h
```

Define global state for L-BFGS-B? It doesn't need caching? Use separate functions.
```
def lbfgs_obj(x):
    val,g=smooth_value_gradient(np.clip(x,0,1), alpha)
    diff=x.sum()-target
    mu=...
    val += 0.5*mu*diff*diff
    g = g + mu*diff*np.ones(n)
    return val,g
```

Implementation of `best_pair_move` using `L` provided:
```
def best_delta_from_coeff(F,L,i,j,lo,hi,M,grid=40, eps=1e-8, off=...):
   ds = np.linspace(lo,hi,grid)
   if lo < 0 < hi: ds = np.unique(np.concatenate(([0.0], ds)))
   d2=ds*ds
   Y = F[None,:] + L[None,:]*ds
   Y[off] -= 2*d2
   Y[off+i-j] += d2
   Y[off+j-i] += d2
   vals=Y.max(axis=1)
   idx = int(np.argmin(vals))
   if vals[idx] >= M - eps:
       return None
   # refine around idx
   for _ in range(2):
       l = max(0, idx-2); r=min(len(ds)-1, idx+2)
       a=ds[l]; b=ds[r]
       if b-a <1e-15: break
       ds2=np.linspace(a,b,grid)
       d2=ds2*ds2
       Y=...
       vals2=Y.max(axis=1)
       idx2=argmin
       if vals2[idx2] < M-eps:
          ds=ds2; idx=idx2
       else: break
   d=ds[idx]; val=vals[idx]
   if val < M - eps:
       return d,val
   return None
```
If best at endpoint 0 (δ=0) not improvement. If best at endpoints lo/hi with improvement, refine interval adjacent endpoints? If idx=0, l=0,r=2 covers [lo, ds[2]] maybe. Good. But if true min at endpoint lo, coarse best at lo. Refine around [lo,ds[2]] won't move lower. Fine, endpoint candidate already evaluated. But if best at endpoint and need exact, can evaluate lo/hi explicitly. We'll include ds includes endpoints. Good.

Potential issue if lo=hi? skip. If lo<0<hi, we insert 0; after `np.unique`, lengths maybe grid+1.

Eps: `eps_count = max(1e-10, 1e-9 / dx?)` Wait M counts. Use C threshold: `min_improve_c` passed. Convert: `eps_count = min_improve_c / dx`. For n=250 dx=0.008, 1e-9/0.008=1.25e-7. Good. Use maybe `min_improve_c=1e-8` ->1.25e-6. To catch 1e-4 total, okay. In pair_search, current M may be bestC/dx. Use `eps = max(1e-10, min_improve_c/dx)`. If no move >eps, stop; but many tiny moves could sum. Perhaps use smaller 1e-10 c ->1e-8 count. Let's set `min_improve_c=1e-10` in pair_search. Then eps_count=1.25e-8. Could accept many tiny. During SLSQP, c improvements not incremental. Fine.

After updating F, due to rounding, `F.max()` may be slightly different from `val` computed. We can set `M = float(F.max())` after update. If `M*dx < bestC` update. If val < M? ok.

When updating h, due to δ at bounds, after `h[i] += d`, `h[j] -= d`, clip values to [0,1]. If d at bound, may be exact but rounding. Use:

```

Function `lbfgs_smooth`:
```
def lbfgs_smooth(h,n,alpha,mu,maxiter,deadline):
  h=project_box_sum(h.copy(),n/2)
  _STATE.update(mode='lbfgs', alpha=alpha, mu=mu, n=n, target=n/2, deadline=deadline, last_x=None)
  bounds=[(0,1)]*n
  try:
    res = minimize(opt_fun, h, method='L-BFGS-B', jac=opt_jac, bounds=bounds, options={'maxiter':maxiter,'ftol':1e-12,'gtol':1e-8}, callback=_timeout_callback)
  except: res=None
  if res and res.x is not None: h=res.x
  return project_box_sum(h,n/2)
```
Custom deadline: callback returns True if time > deadline. But if starting, it may do one iteration beyond. Good.
Potential cache with mode: In `opt_fun`, if x same as last_x but mode changed? We set last_x=None before each. Good.

`slsqp_smooth` similar with equality. For SLSQP, bounds and constraint. Use `jac=opt_jac` but opt_jac includes penalty if mode='slsqp' no. Set mode slsqp, mu 0. Constraint separate. SLSQP may call opt_fun with x and opt_jac; cache okay.

### Detailed Functions

#### compute_counts and C
```
def erdos_counts(h):
    return np.correlate(h, 1.0 - h, mode='full')
def compute_c5(h,n):
    f=erdos_counts(h)
    return float(np.max(f) * (2.0/n))
```

#### project_box_sum
Bisection. Need ensure high/low bracket. Since h in [0,1], λ in [-1,1]. If h sum > target, solution λ maybe positive; if sum < target, λ negative. Bisection:
```
def project_box_sum(x,target):
    x=np.asarray(x,dtype=float)
    x=np.clip(x,0,1)
    s=x.sum()
    if abs(s-target) <= 1e-13:
       # adjust residual exactly maybe
       if s != target:
          # find slack
       return x
    lo=-1.0; hi=1.0
    # g(λ) = sum(clip(x-λ)); g decreasing
    for _ in range(80):
       mid=(lo+hi)/2
       val=np.sum(np.clip(x-mid,0,1))
       if val > target:
          lo=mid
       else:
          hi=mid
    lam=(lo+hi)/2
    y=np.clip(x-lam,0,1)
    diff=target-y.sum()
    if abs(diff) > 1e-14:
       # adjust largest slack/weight
       if diff > 0:
          idx=int(np.argmax(1.0-y))
          add=min(1.0-y[idx], diff)
          y[idx]+=add
          diff-=add
          if diff>0:
             # distribute small to others? Should not happen
             room=np.where((1.0-y)>0)[0]
             for idx in room:
               add=min(1.0-y[idx], diff)
               y[idx]+=add; diff-=add
               if diff<=0: break
       else:
          idx=int(np.argmax(y))
          sub=min(y[idx], -diff)
          y[idx]-=sub; diff+=sub
          if diff<0:
             room=np.where(y>0)[0]
             for idx in room:
               sub=min(y[idx], -diff)
               y[idx]-=sub; diff+=sub
               if diff>=0: break
    return y
```
But bisection with 80 it exact to ~1e-24, diff small. Good.

For n even target integer; n odd .5. `np.sum` double.

#### smooth_value_gradient
```
def weighted_gradient(h,w): loop lags
def smooth_value_gradient(h,alpha):
   F=np.correlate(h,1-h,'full')
   m=F.max()
   z=alpha*(F-m)
   ez=np.exp(z)
   s=ez/ez.sum()
   val=m + np.log(ez.sum())/alpha
   g=weighted_gradient(h,s)
   return float(val), g
```
If alpha =0? not.

weighted_gradient implementation:
```
def weighted_gradient(h,w):
   n=len(h); g=np.zeros(n); off=n-1
   for idx in range(len(w)):
       wl=float(w[idx])
       if wl <= 1e-15: continue
       l=idx-off
       if l > 0:
           m=n-l
           # slices
           g[:m] -= wl * h[l:]
           g[l:] += wl * (1.0 - h[:m])
       elif l == 0:
           g += wl * (1.0 - 2.0*h)
       else:
           m=n+l  # l negative
           start=-l
           g[start:] -= wl * h[:m]
           g[:m] += wl * (1.0 - h[start:])
   return g
```
Check for l<0: n+l positive. Eg n=5,l=-2,m=3,start=2. g[2:] length3 -= h[:3]; g[:3] += 1-h[2:]. good.

#### delta_array(k,h)
```
def delta_array(k,h):
   n=len(h); L=2*n-1; off=n-1; d=np.zeros(L)
   # complement: l from -k to n-1-k
   l0=max(-off, -k); l1=min(off, n-1-k)
   if l0 <= l1:
       idx=np.arange(l0+off, l1+off+1)
       d[idx] -= h[k + (idx - off)]
   # h factor: l from k-off to k
   l0=max(-off, k-off); l1=min(off, k)
   if l0 <= l1:
       idx=np.arange(l0+off, l1+off+1)
       d[idx] += 1.0 - h[k - (idx - off)]
   return d
```
Need ensure index arrays not huge? n small. For complement, k+(idx-off) = k+l within [0,n-1]. For hfactor, k-l within [0,n-1]. Good.

#### best_pair_delta
```
def best_pair_delta(F,Lvec,i,j,lo,hi,M,off,eps,grid=40):
    ds=np.linspace(lo,hi,grid)
    if lo < 0.0 < hi:
       ds=np.unique(np.concatenate(([0.0], ds)))
    else:
       ds=np.unique(ds)
    # ensure 0 if within interval? If interval all negative or positive, no need but if 0 at endpoint perhaps not? If lo=0, linspace includes 0. If hi=0, includes 0.
    best_d=ds[0]; best_val=float('inf')
    for _ in range(2):
       d2=ds*ds
       Y = F[None,:] + Lvec[None,:]*ds
       Y[off] -= 2.0*d2
       Y[off + i - j] += d2
       Y[off + j - i] += d2
       vals=np.max(Y, axis=0)
       idx=int(np.argmin(vals))
       if vals[idx] < best_val:
          best_val=float(vals[idx]); best_d=float(ds[idx])
       if best_val >= M - eps:
          return None
       # refine interval around idx
       a_idx=max(0, idx-2); b_idx=min(len(ds)-1, idx+2)
       a=float(ds[a_idx]); b=float(ds[b_idx])
       if b - a < 1e-15:
          # if at endpoint, expand one side? If idx==0, b=ds[1]; if a=lo, still. If b-a small but not at true endpoint? fine.
          if idx > 0:
             a=float(ds[max(0,idx-4)]); b=float(ds[min(len(ds)-1,idx)])
          elif idx < len(ds)-1:
             a=float(ds[idx]); b=float(ds[min(len(ds)-1,idx+4)])
          else:
             break
          if b-a <1e-15: break
       ds=np.linspace(a,b,grid)
       if lo < 0.0 < hi:
          # ensure 0 in refined if within? initial enough. But if refined doesn't contain 0 and best was not 0, no need. If best at 0, interval centered includes 0. We can add 0 if lo<=0<=hi and not close?
       # maybe recompute; if 0 within a,b, add? do unique with 0.
       if lo <= 0.0 <= hi:
          ds=np.unique(np.concatenate(([0.0], ds)))
    if best_val < M - eps:
       return best_d, best_val
    return None
```
Need be careful `Y[off + i - j]` if same as off? i=j skip. If i-j could make index out? off ±(n-1) within 0..2n-2. Good.
If `off+i-j` equals `off+j-i`? Only if i=j. Good.
If off = index and cross also? if i-j=0 no.

#### pair_search
We'll implement with `max_moves` maybe 50000.
```
def pair_search(h, deadline, rng=None, max_moves=50000, min_improve_c=1e-10, grid=40, alpha_w=40.0):
    if rng is None: rng=np.random.RandomState(12345)
    n=len(h); target=n/2; dx=2.0/n; off=n-1
    h=project_box_sum(h.copy(),target)
    F=erdos_counts(h)
    M=float(F.max()); C=M*dx
    best_h=h.copy(); best_C=C; best_F=F.copy()
    moves=0; no_improve=0
    epsC=min_improve_c
    eps=epsC/dx
    eps=max(eps,1e-9)
    while time.time() < deadline and moves < max_moves:
       # gradient weights
       z=alpha_w*(F-M)
       ez=np.exp(z); w=ez/ez.sum()
       g=weighted_gradient(h,w)
       order=np.argsort(g)
       P=6
       low=order[:P]; high=order[-P:][::-1]
       cand=[]
       for ii in low:
          for jj in high:
             if ii != jj: cand.append((int(ii),int(jj)))
       # random based on g
       for _ in range(10):
          ii=int(order[rng.randint(0, max(1,P+10))])
          jj=int(order[rng.randint(n-P-10, n-P-1)])
          if ii != jj: cand.append((ii,jj))
       # unique keep order
       if not cand: cand=[(0,1)]
       seen=set(); uniq_cand=[]
       for pair in cand:
          if pair not in seen:
             seen.add(pair); uniq_cand.append(pair)
       # delta cache for unique variables
       vars_list=np.unique(np.array([p[0] for p in uniq_cand]+[p[1] for p in uniq_cand]))
       delta_cache={}
       for kk in vars_list: delta_cache[int(kk)]=delta_array(int(kk),h)
       improved=False
       for ii,jj in uniq_cand:
          if time.time() > deadline: break
          hi=min(h[jj],1.0-h[ii]); lo=max(-h[ii],h[jj]-1.0)
          if hi-lo < 1e-13: continue
          Lvec=delta_cache[ii]-delta_cache[jj]
          res=best_pair_delta(F,Lvec,ii,jj,lo,hi,M,off,eps,grid=grid)
          if res is None: continue
          d,val=res
          # update
          oldi=h[ii]; oldj=h[jj]
          h[ii]=oldi+d; h[jj]=oldj-d
          # clip tolerance
          if h[ii] < 0: h[ii]=0.0
          elif h[ii] > 1: h[ii]=1.0
          if h[jj] < 0: h[jj]=0.0
          elif h[jj] > 1: h[jj]=1.0
          # update F
          F = F + Lvec*d + 0.0
          F[off] -= 2.0*d*d
          F[off+ii-jj] += d*d
          F[off+jj-ii] += d*d
          M_new=float(F.max())
          # If clipping invalid? if abs(h[ii]+? ) inconsistent recompute
          if abs(h[ii]-(oldi+d))>1e-12 or abs(h[jj]-(oldj-d))>1e-12:
              h=project_box_sum(h,target); F=erdos_counts(h); M_new=float(F.max())
          M=M_new
          moves += 1
          if M*dx < best_C - 1e-12:
              best_C=M*dx; best_h=h.copy(); best_F=F.copy()
          if moves % 25 == 0:
              h=project_box_sum(h,target)
              F=erdos_counts(h); M=float(F.max()); best_C=min(best_C,M*dx); if M*dx<best_C? update best_h if equal?
          improved=True
          break
       if improved:
          no_improve=0
       else:
          no_improve += 1
          if no_improve > 15:
             # random candidates not gradient
             for _ in range(80):
                if time.time()>deadline: break
                ii=rng.randint(n); jj=rng.randint(n)
                if ii==jj: continue
                hi=min(h[jj],1.0-h[ii]); lo=max(-h[ii],h[jj]-1.0)
                if hi-lo<1e-13: continue
                Lvec=delta_array(ii,h)-delta_array(jj,h)
                res=best_pair_delta(...)
                if res: apply... break
             if improved: no_improve=0
          if no_improve > 60:
             # perturb
             improved=False
             for _ in range(30):
                ii=rng.randint(n); jj=rng.randint(n)
                if ii==jj: continue
                hi=min(h[jj],1.0-h[ii]); lo=max(-h[ii],h[jj]-1.0)
                if hi-lo <1e-6: continue
                d=(hi-lo)*(rng.uniform(0.0,0.3) - 0.15) # small centered
                # ensure d within interval
                if d < lo: d=lo+ (hi-lo)*0.1? maybe
                h2=h.copy(); h2[ii]+=d; h2[jj]-=d; h2=project_box_sum(h2,target)
                F2=erdos_counts(h2); C2=float(F2.max()*dx)
                if C2 <= best_C + 1e-5 and C2 <= C + 1e-4:
                   h=h2; F=F2; M=C2/dx; C=M*dx; no_improve=0
                   improved=True
                   break
             if not improved:
                # if no perturbation works, break local
                if no_improve > 200:
                   break
    # final project and exact
    hbest=best_h.copy() # But current h may have improved but not recorded? We update whenever < best_C - 1e-12. At end, also evaluate current.
    h_cur=project_box_sum(h,target); C_cur=compute_c5(h_cur,n)
    if C_cur < best_C:
       best_C=C_cur; best_h=h_cur
    best_h=project_box_sum(best_h,target); best_C=compute_c5(best_h,n)
    return best_h,best_C
```
Need improve update: If M*dx < best_C, update. But if best_C initialized C. After many moves, current C may equal best. Good.
When recomputing every 25, if M*dx < best_C update best_h. Need not modify code with inline if? We'll write proper.
Potential bug: in random perturbation, C2 variable counts? Use `float(np.max(F2)*dx)`.
`eps` min improve count. If best_C maybe not current M? We use current M threshold. If current has worsened due to perturbation, it may accept moves from current to improve; best preserved.

Time in pair_search: candidate delta cache uses `delta_array` for random 80 variables maybe each length 499; fine. But in no_improve random 80, computing delta and grid each could be 80*24k=2M; if no_improve cycles many, expensive. Limit: when no_improve 15, do random pairs once; if still no, perturb; if no_improve > 200 break. So max a few.

### LBFGS and SLSQP caches with callback
Top-level `_STATE = {}`
```
def _smooth_cache(x, alpha):
    val,g=smooth_value_gradient(x,alpha)
    _STATE['last_x']=x.copy(); _STATE['last_val']=val; _STATE['last_g']=g
    return val,g
def _opt_fun(x):
    alpha=_STATE['alpha']; mode=_STATE['mode']
    val,g=_smooth_cache(x, alpha)
    if mode=='lbfgs':
       diff=x.sum()-_STATE['target']; mu=_STATE['mu']
       if diff !=0.0:
          val += 0.5*mu*diff*diff
          g = g + (mu*diff)*np.ones(_STATE['n'])
    _STATE['last_x']=x.copy(); _STATE['last_val']=val; _STATE['last_g']=g
    return val
def _opt_jac(x):
    if 'last_x' in _STATE and _STATE['last_x'] is not None:
       if x.shape == _STATE['last_x'].shape and np.allclose(x, _STATE['last_x'], rtol=0.0, atol=1e-15):
          return _STATE['last_g']
    alpha=...
    val,g=_smooth_cache(x,alpha)
    if mode lbfgs: diff..., g =...
    _STATE['last_x']=x.copy(); _STATE['last_g']=g
    return g
def _cons_fun(x): return x.sum()-_STATE['target']
def _timeout_cb(x): return time.time() > _STATE.get('deadline',0.0)
```
In `_opt_jac`, after `_smooth_cache` it stores last_g for smooth, but if lbfgs need add penalty; update `_STATE['last_g']`. `_smooth_cache` already stores, then we override. OK.

It might pass same x? Not guaranteed. Use `np.array_equal`? For floats, if recompute due to line search, x differs. We can skip cache and just compute both every time? ```
chk = float(np.dot(x, np.arange(n)*1000003.0)) maybe not unique. allclose fine.
```

def lbfgs_smooth(h,n,alpha,mu,maxiter,deadline):
    target=n/2; h=project_box_sum(h.copy(),target)
    _STATE.update(alpha=alpha, target=target, n=n, mu=mu, mode='lbfgs', deadline=deadline, last_x=None)
    bounds=[(0.0,1.0)]*n
    try:
       res=minimize(_opt_fun,h,jac=_opt_jac,bounds=bounds,method='L-BFGS-B',options={'maxiter':maxiter,'ftol':1e-12,'gtol':1e-8},callback=_timeout_cb)
       if res is not None and res.x is not None: h=res.x
    except Exception: pass

def slsqp_smooth(h,n,alpha,maxiter,deadline):
   target=n/2; h=project...

   bounds=[(0,1)]*n; cons={'type':'eq','fun':_cons_fun}

def improve_candidate(h,n,deadline,rng,idx):
    h=project_box_sum(h.copy(),n/2)
    best_h=h.copy(); best_C=compute_c5(h,n)
    t=time.time()
    # Phase shortest: pair search 40%
    dl=min(deadline, t + max(1.0, 0.45*(deadline-t)))
    h1,C1=pair_search(h,dl,rng, max_moves=50000, min_improve_c=1e-10, grid=40)
    if C1<best_C: update
    # If time remains
    if time.time() < deadline - 20:
      # lbfgs maybe 15s
      dl2=min(deadline, time.time()+max(2,0.2*(deadline-time.time())))
      h2=lbfgs_smooth(h1,n,alpha=100.0,mu=100.0,maxiter=400,deadline=dl2)
      C2=compute_c5; update
      # slsqp exact 10s
      if time.time() < deadline - 10:
         dl3=min(deadline, time.time()+max(2,0.15*(deadline-time.time())))
         h3=slsqp_smooth(h2,n,alpha=500.0,maxiter=200,deadline=dl3)
         C3=eval; update
    # final pair search until deadline-2
    if time.time() < deadline - 1:
       h4,C4=pair_search(best_h, deadline-1, rng, max_moves=200000, min_improve_c=1e-10, grid=48)
       if C4<best_C: update
    return best_h,best_C
```

### Time of weighted_gradient in pair_search
Each sweep 499 loops over floats, each with slices. If 1000 sweeps, ~500k loops; each loop with 2 slice operations, maybe 2µs? Actually Python loop overhead plus numpy allocation of slice. Could be 0.1ms per loop? 499*0.1ms=50ms per gradient. 1000 ->50s. Might be high. We can optimize gradient using convolution/correlation with weights to reduce Python loops. Weighted gradient:
g[k] = ∑_{l} w_l * [1_{k-l valid} (1-h_{k-l}) - 1_{k+l valid} h_{k+l}]
Term1: g_h[k] = (w_l * (1-h_{k-l})) over valid. This is cross-correlation between w (length 2n-1) and (1-h) (length n). Let's compute using `np.correlate(1-h, w, 'full')` or `np.convolve`. Need exact indexing.
Define w_l for l=-(n-1)..n-1. Need for k, sum_l w_l a_{k-l} where a=1-h, valid 0≤k-l<n. This is (a cross-correlated with w?) `np.correlate(a, w, 'full')` outputs over k? Let's test small. If `correlate(a,w)` with a len n, v len 2n-1, using formula c[off?] maybe. We want g1_index? Easier to test conceptually but no runtime. We can implement via FFT? Not necessary maybe.
Maybe use `scipy.signal.fftconvolve`? scipy.signal allowed? Use scipy? We imported numpy only. Could use `np.convolve`.
Let's derive with `np.correlate(a, v)`: output index off_a+v? earlier formula c[off + k] = ∑ a[n+k] v[n]? For a length m, v length l. For a=1-h length n, v=w length L. If output index? This is cross-correlation of w with a at lag k? Specifically c[k] = Σ_p w[p?]? If using `np.correlate(w, a)`: c[k]=Σ_p w[p+k] a[p]. Not. `np.correlate(a, w)`: c[k]=Σ_p a[p+k] w[p]. Substitute p=k? Let l=-p? Hmm.

### Could optimize delta_array with precomputed index arrays per k?

#### project_box_sum adjustment loop
```
def project_box_sum(x, target):
    x = np.asarray(x, dtype=float)
    if x.size == 0: return x
    x = np.clip(x, 0.0, 1.0)
    if abs(x.sum() - target) <= 1e-14:
        # still exact adjust? skip
        pass
    lo = -1.0; hi = 1.0
    # Ensure bracket: if sum(clip(x-lo)) < target? lo=-1 gives all 1 sum n. hi=1 gives 0.
    for _ in range(80):
       mid = 0.5*(lo+hi)
       val = np.sum(np.clip(x - mid, 0.0, 1.0))
       if val > target:
           lo = mid
       else:
           hi = mid
    lam=0.5*(lo+hi)
    y=np.clip(x-lam, 0.0,1.0)
    diff=target - y.sum()
    if abs(diff) > 1e-15:
       if diff > 0:
          # add to all with room proportionally? simpler one by one
          room = 1.0 - y
          if room.sum() <= 0: return y # should not
          # If diff small, add to argmax room
          idx = int(np.argmax(room))
          add = min(room[idx], diff)
          y[idx] += add
          diff -= add
          while diff > 1e-16:
             room = 1.0 - y
             if room.sum() <= 0: break
             idx = int(np.argmax(room))
             add = min(room[idx], diff)
             y[idx] += add
             diff -= add
             if add <=0: break
       else:
          sub = min(y.sum(), -diff)
          idx = int(np.argmax(y))
          y[idx] -= min(y[idx], -diff)
          diff += min(y[idx]? careful)
```
This while with recompute each diff maybe small. Could do one adjustment; diff after bisection tiny. Simpler:
```
if diff != 0:
  if diff>0:
    idx=np.argmax(1-y); y[idx]=min(1,y[idx]+diff)
  else:
    idx=np.argmax(y); y[idx]=max(0,y[idx]+diff)
  # If still diff due to bound, distribute over non-bound
  diff2 = target - y.sum()
  if abs(diff2)>0:
     if diff2>0:
       mask=(y<1); if mask.any(): y[mask]+=diff2/mask.sum(); y=np.clip(y,0,1)
     else:
       mask=(y>0); if mask.any(): y[mask]+=diff2/mask.sum(); y=np.clip(y,0,1)
```
After adding to non-bound, sum may again off due to clipping if diff2 large (not). Good.
We'll implement robust.

### `resample_h`
```
def resample_h(h,n_old,n_new):
   if n_new == n_old: return h.copy()
   xo=np.linspace(0.0,2.0,n_old)
   xn=np.linspace(0.0,2.0,n_new)
   return np.interp(xn,xo,h)
```

### Let's adjust `improve_candidate` phases:
```
def improve_candidate(h,n,deadline,rng):
    t0c=time.time(); remaining=deadline-t0c
    best_h=h; best_C=compute
    # pair1: min(45%, 40s? Actually allocate 0.45 remaining)
    dl1=min(deadline, t0c + 0.45*remaining)
    h1,C1=pair_search(h,dl1,rng,max_moves=20000,grid=44)
    update
    # smooth if n<=400 and enough
    if n <= 400 and time.time() < deadline - 30:
       rem=deadline-time.time()
       # LBFGS 30% rem
       dl2=min(deadline, time.time() + 0.35*rem)
       h2=lbfgs_smooth(h1,n,150,200,800,dl2)
       C2...; update
       if time.time() < deadline - 15:
          dl3=min(deadline, time.time() + 0.25*(deadline-time.time()))
          h3=slsqp_smooth(best_h,n,800,200,dl3)
          C3 update
    # final pair with remaining
    if time.time() < deadline - 1:
       h4,C4=pair_search(best_h, deadline, rng, max_moves=100000, grid=48)
    return best_h,best_C
```
But if first pair1 takes 45%, LBFGS 35% of remaining (15% total), SLSQP 12.5%, final pair 27. Good.

def append_candidate(cands,h,n):
    hh=project_box_sum(np.asarray(h,float),int(n)/2.0)
    c=compute_c5(hh,int(n))
    cands.append((c,hh,int(n)))

Project implementation final:
```
def project_box_sum(x,target):
    x=np.asarray(x,dtype=float)
    n=x.size
    if n==0: return x
    x=np.clip(x,0.0,1.0)
    lo=-1.0; hi=1.0
    for _ in range(80):
       mid=0.5*(lo+hi)
       val=np.sum(np.clip(x-mid,0.0,1.0))
       if val > target: lo=mid
       else: hi=mid
    y=np.clip(x-0.5*(lo+hi),0.0,1.0)
    diff=target - y.sum()
    if abs(diff) > 1e-15:
       if diff > 0:
          mask = y < 1.0
          if mask.any():
             room = 1.0 - y
             # distribute equally among mask to avoid bound issues
             y[mask] += diff / float(mask.sum())
             y = np.clip(y,0.0,1.0)
          else:
             idx=int(np.argmax(room if 'room' in locals() else 1.0-y)); y[idx]=1.0; diff=target-y.sum(); if diff>0... 
       else: ...
    # second pass
    diff2=target-y.sum()
    if abs(diff2)>1e-15:
       if diff2>0:
          idx=int(np.argmax(1.0-y)); y[idx]=min(1.0,y[idx]+diff2)
       else:
          idx=int(np.argmax(y)); y[idx]=max(0.0,y[idx]+diff2)
    return y
```
When distributing diff among mask, if diff is large maybe some hit 1; but target feasible and bisection λ ensures diff small. Good. For negative diff, distribute among mask y>0.
But `mask.sum()` could be 0 if all at 1 and need add, impossible if target≤n. If all at 0 and need subtract, impossible. Good.
Potential if after distribution clipping causes sum off >1e-12, second single adjust might also hit bound; but tiny.

```python
import numpy as np
import time
from scipy.optimize import minimize

_STATE = {}


def erdos_counts(h):
    return np.correlate(h, 1.0 - h, mode="full")


def compute_c5(h, n):
    h = np.asarray(h, dtype=float)
    n = int(n)
    vals = erdos_counts(h)
    return float(np.max(vals) * (2.0 / n))


def project_box_sum(x, target):
    x = np.asarray(x, dtype=float)
    n = x.size
    if n == 0:
        return x
    x = np.clip(x, 0.0, 1.0)
    lo = -1.0
    hi = 1.0
    for _ in range(80):
        mid = 0.5 * (lo + hi)
        val = np.sum(np.clip(x - mid, 0.0, 1.0))
        if val > target:
            lo = mid
        else:
            hi = mid
    lam = 0.5 * (lo + hi)
    y = np.clip(x - lam, 0.0, 1.0)
    diff = target - y.sum()
    if abs(diff) > 1e-15:
        if diff > 0:
            mask = y < 1.0
            if mask.any():
                y[mask] += diff / float(mask.sum())
        else:
            mask = y > 0.0
            if mask.any():
                y[mask] += diff / float(mask.sum())
        y = np.clip(y, 0.0, 1.0)
    diff = target - y.sum()
    if abs(diff) > 1e-15:
        if diff > 0:
            idx = int(np.argmax(1.0 - y))
            y[idx] = min(1.0, y[idx] + diff)
        else:
            idx = int(np.argmax(y))
            y[idx] = max(0.0, y[idx] + diff)
    return y


def weighted_gradient(h, w):
    n = len(h)
    g = np.zeros(n, dtype=float)
    off = n - 1
    L = len(w)
    for idx in range(L):
        wl = float(w[idx])
        if wl <= 1e-15:
            continue
        l = idx - off
        if l > 0:
            m = n - l
            g[:m] -= wl * h[l:]
            g[l:] += wl * (1.0 - h[:m])
        elif l == 0:
            g += wl * (1.0 - 2.0 * h)
        else:
            m = n + l
            start = -l
            g[start:] -= wl * h[:m]
            g[:m] += wl * (1.0 - h[start:])
    return g


def smooth_value_gradient(h, alpha):
    F = erdos_counts(h)
    m = float(np.max(F))
    z = alpha * (F - m)
    ez = np.exp(z)
    s = ez / ez.sum()
    val = m + np.log(ez.sum()) / alpha
    g = weighted_gradient(h, s)
    return float(val), g


def delta_array(k, h):
    n = len(h)
    off = n - 1
    L = 2 * n - 1
    d = np.zeros(L, dtype=float)

    l0 = max(-off, -k)
    l1 = min(off, n - 1 - k)
    if l0 <= l1:
        idx = np.arange(l0 + off, l1 + off + 1)
        d[idx] -= h[k + (idx - off)]

    l0 = max(-off, k - off)
    l1 = min(off, k)
    if l0 <= l1:
        idx = np.arange(l0 + off, l1 + off + 1)
        d[idx] += 1.0 - h[k - (idx - off)]

    return d


def best_pair_delta(F, Lvec, i, j, lo, hi, M, off, eps, grid=44):
    if hi <= lo:
        return None
    ds = np.linspace(lo, hi, grid)
    if lo < 0.0 < hi:
        ds = np.unique(np.concatenate((np.array([0.0]), ds)))
    else:
        ds = np.unique(ds)
    if ds.size == 0:
        return None

    best_d = float(ds[0])
    best_val = float("inf")

    for _ in range(2):
        d2 = ds * ds
        Y = F[None, :] + Lvec[None, :] * ds
        Y[off] -= 2.0 * d2
        Y[off + i - j] += d2
        Y[off + j - i] += d2
        vals = np.max(Y, axis=0)
        idx = int(np.argmin(vals))
        if vals[idx] < best_val:
            best_val = float(vals[idx])
            best_d = float(ds[idx])
        if best_val >= M - eps:
            return None

        a_idx = max(0, idx - 2)
        b_idx = min(len(ds) - 1, idx + 2)
        a = float(ds[a_idx])
        b = float(ds[b_idx])
        if b - a < 1e-15:
            if idx > 0:
                a = float(ds[max(0, idx - 4)])
                b = float(ds[min(len(ds) - 1, idx)])
            elif idx < len(ds) - 1:
                a = float(ds[idx])
                b = float(ds[min(len(ds) - 1, idx + 4)])
            else:
                break
            if b - a < 1e-15:
                break
        ds = np.linspace(a, b, grid)
        if lo <= 0.0 <= hi:
            ds = np.unique(np.concatenate((np.array([0.0]), ds)))

    if best_val < M - eps:
        return best_d, best_val
    return None


def pair_search(h, deadline, rng=None, max_moves=30000, min_improve_c=1e-9, grid=44, alpha_w=50.0):
    if rng is None:
        rng = np.random.RandomState(123456789)
    h = np.asarray(h, dtype=float)
    n = len(h)
    target = n / 2.0
    dx = 2.0 / n
    off = n - 1

    h = project_box_sum(h.copy(), target)
    F = erdos_counts(h)
    M = float(np.max(F))
    C = M * dx
    best_h = h.copy()
    best_C = C

    moves = 0
    no_improve = 0
    escape_count = 0
    since_grad = 10
    g = None
    order = None
    P = 7

    eps = max(min_improve_c / dx, 1e-10)

    while time.time() < deadline and moves < max_moves:
        if g is None or since_grad >= 10 or (no_improve > 0 and no_improve % 5 == 0):
            z = alpha_w * (F - M)
            ez = np.exp(z)
            w = ez / ez.sum()
            g = weighted_gradient(h, w)
            order = np.argsort(g)
            since_grad = 0

        low = order[:P]
        high = order[-P:][::-1]
        cand = []
        for ii in low:
            for jj in high:
                if int(ii) != int(jj):
                    cand.append((int(ii), int(jj)))
        for _ in range(12):
            ii = int(order[rng.randint(0, max(1, P + 10))])
            jj = int(order[rng.randint(n - P - 10, n - P - 1)])
            if ii != jj:
                cand.append((ii, jj))

        seen = set()
        uniq_cand = []
        for pair in cand:
            if pair not in seen:
                seen.add(pair)
                uniq_cand.append(pair)
        if not uniq_cand:
            uniq_cand = [(0, 1)]

        vars_list = np.unique(np.array([p[0] for p in uniq_cand] + [p[1] for p in uniq_cand]))
        delta_cache = {}
        for kk in vars_list:
            delta_cache[int(kk)] = delta_array(int(kk), h)

        improved = False
        for ii, jj in uniq_cand:
            if time.time() > deadline:
                break
            hi = min(h[jj], 1.0 - h[ii])
            lo = max(-h[ii], h[jj] - 1.0)
            if hi - lo < 1e-13:
                continue
            Lvec = delta_cache[ii] - delta_cache[jj]
            res = best_pair_delta(F, Lvec, ii, jj, lo, hi, M, off, eps, grid=grid)
            if res is None:
                continue
            d, val = res
            old_i = h[ii]
            old_j = h[jj]
            h[ii] = old_i + d
            h[jj] = old_j - d
            if h[ii] < 0.0:
                h[ii] = 0.0
            elif h[ii] > 1.0:
                h[ii] = 1.0
            if h[jj] < 0.0:
                h[jj] = 0.0
            elif h[jj] > 1.0:
                h[jj] = 1.0

            F = F + Lvec * d
            F[off] -= 2.0 * d * d
            F[off + ii - jj] += d * d
            F[off + jj - ii] += d * d
            M_new = float(np.max(F))

            if abs(h[ii] - (old_i + d)) > 1e-12 or abs(h[jj] - (old_j - d)) > 1e-12:
                h = project_box_sum(h, target)
                F = erdos_counts(h)
                M_new = float(np.max(F))

            M = M_new
            C = M * dx
            moves += 1
            if C < best_C - 1e-13:
                best_C = C
                best_h = h.copy()

            if moves % 25 == 0:
                h = project_box_sum(h, target)
                F = erdos_counts(h)
                M = float(np.max(F))
                C = M * dx
                if C < best_C:
                    best_C = C
                    best_h = h.copy()

            improved = True
            since_grad += 1
            break

        if improved:
            no_improve = 0
            continue

        no_improve += 1

        if no_improve == 10:
            for _ in range(60):
                if time.time() > deadline:
                    break
                ii = int(rng.randint(n))
                jj = int(rng.randint(n))
                if ii == jj:
                    continue
                hi = min(h[jj], 1.0 - h[ii])
                lo = max(-h[ii], h[jj] - 1.0)
                if hi - lo < 1e-13:
                    continue
                Lvec = delta_array(ii, h) - delta_array(jj, h)
                res = best_pair_delta(F, Lvec, ii, jj, lo, hi, M, off, eps, grid=grid)
                if res is None:
                    continue
                d, val = res
                old_i = h[ii]
                old_j = h[jj]
                h[ii] = old_i + d
                h[jj] = old_j - d
                h[ii] = min(1.0, max(0.0, h[ii]))
                h[jj] = min(1.0, max(0.0, h[jj]))
                F = F + Lvec * d
                F[off] -= 2.0 * d * d
                F[off + ii - jj] += d * d
                F[off + jj - ii] += d * d
                M = float(np.max(F))
                C = M * dx
                moves += 1
                if C < best_C - 1e-13:
                    best_C = C
                    best_h = h.copy()
                improved = True
                since_grad += 1
                break
            if improved:
                no_improve = 0
                continue

        if no_improve >= 10:
            accepted = False
            if escape_count < 3:
                for _ in range(40):
                    if time.time() > deadline:
                        break
                    ii = int(rng.randint(n))
                    jj = int(rng.randint(n))
                    if ii == jj:
                        continue
                    hi = min(h[jj], 1.0 - h[ii])
                    lo = max(-h[ii], h[jj] - 1.0)
                    if hi - lo < 1e-4:
                        continue
                    d = (hi - lo) * rng.uniform(0.0, 0.25)
                    if rng.rand() < 0.5:
                        d = -d
                    d = min(max(d, lo), hi)
                    if abs(d) < 1e-9:
                        continue
                    h2 = h.copy()
                    h2[ii] += d
                    h2[jj] -= d
                    h2 = project_box_sum(h2, target)
                    C2 = compute_c5(h2, n)
                    if C2 <= best_C + 1e-5 and C2 <= C + 1e-4:
                        h = h2
                        F = erdos_counts(h)
                        M = float(np.max(F))
                        C = M * dx
                        no_improve = 0
                        escape_count += 1
                        since_grad = 10
                        accepted = True
                        break
            if not accepted:
                break

    h_cur = project_box_sum(h, target)
    C_cur = compute_c5(h_cur, n)
    if C_cur < best_C:
        best_C = C_cur
        best_h = h_cur
    best_h = project_box_sum(best_h, target)
    best_C = compute_c5(best_h, n)
    return best_h, best_C


def _smooth_cache(x, alpha):
    val, g = smooth_value_gradient(x, alpha)
    _STATE["last_x"] = np.array(x, dtype=float, copy=True)
    _STATE["last_val"] = float(val)
    _STATE["last_g"] = np.array(g, dtype=float, copy=True)
    return val, g


def _opt_fun(x):
    alpha = float(_STATE["alpha"])
    val, g = _smooth_cache(x, alpha)
    if _STATE["mode"] == "lbfgs":
        diff = float(x.sum()) - float(_STATE["target"])
        mu = float(_STATE.get("mu", 0.0))
        if mu != 0.0 and diff != 0.0:
            val += 0.5 * mu * diff * diff
            g = g + mu * diff
    _STATE["last_x"] = np.array(x, dtype=float, copy=True)
    _STATE["last_val"] = float(val)
    _STATE["last_g"] = np.array(g, dtype=float, copy=True)
    return val


def _opt_jac(x):
    last_x = _STATE.get("last_x", None)
    if last_x is not None and last_x.shape == x.shape:
        if np.allclose(x, last_x, rtol=0.0, atol=1e-15):
            return _STATE["last_g"]
    alpha = float(_STATE["alpha"])
    val, g = _smooth_cache(x, alpha)
    if _STATE["mode"] == "lbfgs":
        diff = float(x.sum()) - float(_STATE["target"])
        mu = float(_STATE.get("mu", 0.0))
        if mu != 0.0 and diff != 0.0:
            g = g + mu * diff
    _STATE["last_x"] = np.array(x, dtype=float, copy=True)
    _STATE["last_g"] = np.array(g, dtype=float, copy=True)
    return _STATE["last_g"]


def _cons_fun(x):
    return float(x.sum()) - float(_STATE["target"])


def _timeout_callback(x):
    return time.time() > float(_STATE.get("deadline", 0.0))


def lbfgs_smooth(h, n, alpha=150.0, mu=100.0, maxiter=600, deadline=None):
    if deadline is None:
        deadline = time.time() + 30.0
    n = int(n)
    target = n / 2.0
    h = project_box_sum(np.asarray(h, dtype=float).copy(), target)
    _STATE.update(
        mode="lbfgs",
        n=n,
        target=float(target),
        alpha=float(alpha),
        mu=float(mu),
        deadline=float(deadline),
        last_x=None,
        last_val=None,
        last_g=None,
    )
    bounds = [(0.0, 1.0)] * n
    try:
        res = minimize(
            _opt_fun,
            h,
            method="L-BFGS-B",
            jac=_opt_jac,
            bounds=bounds,
            options={"maxiter": int(maxiter), "ftol": 1e-12, "gtol": 1e-7},
            callback=_timeout_callback,
        )
        if res is not None and res.x is not None:
            h = res.x
    except Exception:
        pass
    return project_box_sum(h, target)


def slsqp_smooth(h, n, alpha=800.0, maxiter=200, deadline=None):
    if deadline is None:
        deadline = time.time() + 30.0
    n = int(n)
    target = n / 2.0
    h = project_box_sum(np.asarray(h, dtype=float).copy(), target)
    _STATE.update(
        mode="slsqp",
        n=n,
        target=float(target),
        alpha=float(alpha),
        mu=0.0,
        deadline=float(deadline),
        last_x=None,
        last_val=None,
        last_g=None,
    )
    bounds = [(0.0, 1.0)] * n
    constraints = [{"type": "eq", "fun": _cons_fun}]
    try:
        res = minimize(
            _opt_fun,
            h,
            method="SLSQP",
            jac=_opt_jac,
            bounds=bounds,
            constraints=constraints,
            options={"maxiter": int(maxiter), "ftol": 1e-12, "disp": False},
            callback=_timeout_callback,
        )
        if res is not None and res.x is not None:
            h = res.x
    except Exception:
        pass
    return project_box_sum(h, target)


def random_fourier_h(n, rng, strength=0.24):
    n = int(n)
    x = np.linspace(0.0, 2.0, n)
    h = np.full(n, 0.5, dtype=float)
    for k in range(1, 7):
        amp = rng.uniform(0.02, strength) / max(1, np.sqrt(k))
        ph = rng.uniform(0.0, 2.0 * np.pi)
        h += amp * np.cos(k * np.pi * x + ph)
        amp = rng.uniform(0.02, strength) / max(1, np.sqrt(k))
        ph = rng.uniform(0.0, 2.0 * np.pi)
        h += amp * np.sin(k * np.pi * x + ph)
    return project_box_sum(np.clip(h, 0.0, 1.0), n / 2.0)


def beatty_h(n, alpha):
    x = np.linspace(0.0, 2.0, int(n))
    h = np.mod(x * float(alpha), 1.0)
    return project_box_sum(h, n / 2.0)


def initial_construction(n):
    n = int(n)
    rng = np.random.RandomState(12345)
    x = np.linspace(0.0, 2.0, n)
    bases = [
        0.5 + 0.5 * np.cos(np.pi * x),
        0.5 + 0.5 * np.sin(np.pi * x),
        0.5 + 0.5 * np.cos(2.0 * np.pi * x),
        random_fourier_h(n, rng, 0.3),
        beatty_h(n, 0.6180339887498949),
        beatty_h(n, 0.3819660112501051),
    ]
    best_h = None
    best_c = float("inf")
    for b in bases:
        h = project_box_sum(np.clip(b, 0.0, 1.0), n / 2.0)
        c = compute_c5(h, n)
        if c < best_c:
            best_c = c
            best_h = h.copy()
    if best_h is None:
        best_h = project_box_sum(np.full(n, 0.5), n / 2.0)
    return best_h


def resample_h(h, n_old, n_new):
    n_old = int(n_old)
    n_new = int(n_new)
    h = np.asarray(h, dtype=float)
    if n_new == n_old:
        return h.copy()
    xo = np.linspace(0.0, 2.0, n_old)
    xn = np.linspace(0.0, 2.0, n_new)
    return np.interp(xn, xo, h)


def make_record(idx, h, n):
    n = int(n)
    hh = project_box_sum(np.asarray(h, dtype=float), n / 2.0)
    c = compute_c5(hh, n)
    return (float(c), int(idx), hh, n)


def select_top_records(records, k):
    srec = sorted(records)
    out = []
    seen = set()
    for r in srec:
        nn = r[3]
        if nn not in seen:
            out.append(r)
            seen.add(nn)
            if len(out) >= k:
                break
    return out


def ensure_record(records, nn):
    best = None
    for r in records:
        if r[3] == nn:
            if best is None or r[0] < best[0]:
                best = r
    return best


def improve_candidate(h, n, deadline, rng):
    h = project_box_sum(np.asarray(h, dtype=float).copy(), n / 2.0)
    best_h = h.copy()
    best_c = compute_c5(best_h, n)

    t0c = time.time()
    rem = max(0.0, float(deadline) - t0c)
    dl1 = min(float(deadline), t0c + max(2.0, 0.45 * rem))
    if dl1 > t0c + 1.0:
        h1, c1 = pair_search(h, dl1, rng, max_moves=25000, min_improve_c=1e-9, grid=44)
        if c1 < best_c:
            best_c = c1
            best_h = h1.copy()

    if n <= 420 and time.time() < float(deadline) - 30.0:
        rem = max(0.0, float(deadline) - time.time())
        dl2 = min(float(deadline), time.time() + max(2.0, 0.35 * rem))
        if dl2 > time.time() + 1.0:
            h2 = lbfgs_smooth(best_h, n, alpha=160.0, mu=120.0, maxiter=700, deadline=dl2)
            c2 = compute_c5(h2, n)
            if c2 < best_c:
                best_c = c2
                best_h = h2.copy()

        if time.time() < float(deadline) - 20.0:
            rem = max(0.0, float(deadline) - time.time())
            dl3 = min(float(deadline), time.time() + max(2.0, 0.30 * rem))
            if dl3 > time.time() + 1.0:
                h3 = slsqp_smooth(best_h, n, alpha=900.0, maxiter=180, deadline=dl3)
                c3 = compute_c5(h3, n)
                if c3 < best_c:
                    best_c = c3
                    best_h = h3.copy()

    if time.time() < float(deadline) - 1.0:
        h4, c4 = pair_search(best_h, float(deadline), rng, max_moves=80000, min_improve_c=1e-10, grid=52)
        if c4 < best_c:
            best_c = c4
            best_h = h4.copy()

    best_h = project_box_sum(best_h, n)
    best_c = compute_c5(best_h, n)
    return best_h, best_c


def run(seed=42, budget_s=1100, **kwargs):
    t0 = time.time()
    total = float(budget_s)
    end = t0 + max(10.0, total - max(10.0, 0.02 * total))
    rng = np.random.RandomState(int(seed))

    n0 = 250
    try:
        parent = np.asarray(initial_h_values, dtype=float).copy()
        if parent.size > 0:
            n0 = int(parent.size)
            base = parent
        else:
            base = initial_construction(n0)
    except Exception:
        base = initial_construction(n0)

    base = project_box_sum(base, n0 / 2.0)

    recs = []
    idx = 0

    def add_base_variant(v):
        nonlocal_idx = idx
        recs.append(make_record(nonlocal_idx, v, n0))
        return nonlocal_idx

    idx = 0
    recs.append(make_record(idx, base, n0)); idx += 1
    recs.append(make_record(idx, base[::-1], n0)); idx += 1
    recs.append(make_record(idx, 1.0 - base, n0)); idx += 1
    recs.append(make_record(idx, 1.0 - base[::-1], n0)); idx += 1
    recs.append(make_record(idx, 0.5 * (base + base[::-1]), n0)); idx += 1
    recs.append(make_record(idx, 0.5 * (base + (1.0 - base[::-1])), n0)); idx += 1

    for _ in range(3):
        recs.append(make_record(idx, random_fourier_h(n0, rng, 0.26), n0)); idx += 1

    for alpha in (0.6180339887498949, 0.3819660112501051, 0.3333333333333333):
        recs.append(make_record(idx, beatty_h(n0, alpha), n0)); idx += 1

    scan_ns = set()
    for delta in (-100, -80, -60, -45, -30, -20, -15, -10, -5, -3, 0, 3, 5, 10, 15, 20, 30, 45, 60, 80, 100, 150, 200, 300, 400):
        nn = n0 + delta
        if nn >= 40 and nn <= 900:
            scan_ns.add(nn)
    for nn in (80, 100, 120, 150, 180, 200, 220, 240, 250, 260, 280, 300, 320, 360, 400, 450, 500):
        if nn >= 40 and nn <= 900:
            scan_ns.add(nn)

    for nn in sorted(scan_ns):
        vv = resample_h(base, n0, nn)
        recs.append(make_record(idx, vv, nn))
        idx += 1

    top = select_top_records(recs, 3)
    seen_ns = set(r[3] for r in top)
    if n0 not in seen_ns:
        r0 = ensure_record(recs, n0)
        if r0 is not None:
            top.insert(0, r0)
    if len(top) > 3:
        top = top[:3]

    best_record = min(top, key=lambda x: x[0]) if top else recs[0]
    best_h = best_record[2].copy()
    best_n = int(best_record[3])
    best_c = float(best_record[0])

    phase_total = max(1.0, end - t0)
    if len(top) == 1:
        fracs = [1.0]
    elif len(top) == 2:
        fracs = [0.60, 0.40]
    else:
        fracs = [0.52, 0.26, 0.22]

    used_frac = 0.0
    for pi, rec in enumerate(top):
        if time.time() >= end - 35.0:
            break
        frac = fracs[pi] if pi < len(fracs) else 0.1
        expected_used = phase_total * used_frac
        leftover = max(0.0, time.time() - (t0 + expected_used))
        base_alloc = phase_total * frac + leftover
        available = max(0.0, end - time.time() - 10.0)
        alloc = min(base_alloc, available)
        if alloc < 5.0:
            alloc = min(available, 10.0) if available > 5.0 else 0.0
        if alloc <= 0.0:
            break
        dl = time.time() + alloc
        if dl > end - 5.0:
            dl = end - 5.0
        if dl <= time.time() + 0.5:
            break
        h, c = improve_candidate(rec[2], rec[3], dl, rng)
        if c < best_c:
            best_c = c
            best_h = h.copy()
            best_n = int(rec[3])
        used_frac += frac

    variants = [
        best_h,
        best_h[::-1].copy(),
        1.0 - best_h,
        1.0 - best_h[::-1],
        0.5 * (best_h + best_h[::-1]),
        0.5 * (best_h + (1.0 - best_h[::-1])),
    ]
    for vv in variants:
        hh = project_box_sum(vv, best_n / 2.0)
        cc = compute_c5(hh, best_n)
        if cc < best_c:
            best_c = cc
            best_h = hh

    if time.time() < end - 35.0:
        h, c = pair_search(best_h, end - 5.0, rng, max_moves=200000, min_improve_c=1e-10, grid=56)
        if c < best_c:
            best_c = c
            best_h = h.copy()

        variants2 = [
            best_h,
            best_h[::-1].copy(),
            1.0 - best_h,
            0.5 * (best_h + best_h[::-1]),
        ]
        for vv in variants2:
            hh = project_box_sum(vv, best_n / 2.0)
            cc = compute_c5(hh, best_n)
            if cc < best_c:
                best_c = cc
                best_h = hh

    best_h = project_box_sum(best_h, best_n / 2.0)
    best_c = compute_c5(best_h, best_n)
    return best_h, best_c, int(best_n)
```
